Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the mendelian structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 598. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 909 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).
